Urals Oil Falls to Over 3-Month Low — Russian Urals crude fell to below $65 per barrel, the lowest in over three months, tracking international benchmark prices as markets priced in expectations that a US-Iran deal could reopen the Strait of Hormuz and unleash additional supply. Brent and WTI crude prices dropped as traders anticipated the return of Iranian exports and easing pressure on global inventories. The possible interim agreement, expected to allow Tehran to resume oil sales, has already prompted tanker repositioning ahead of a gradual reopening of the strategic waterway. Analysts expect flows to recover slowly due to security concerns and operational limits. At the same time, Russia is shipping near-record crude volumes as Ukrainian strikes on refineries push more barrels into export markets. Russian shipments averaged 3.83 million barrels a day in the four weeks to June 14, the highest pace this year.

【新加坡金融管理局行長：全球股權投資估值高度依賴AI，但相關營收回報仍具很強不確定性】6月17日，在2026陸家嘴論壇“全體大會二：全球金融治理改革與合作”上，新加坡金融管理局行長謝啇真表示，當前全球經濟面臨越來越多衝擊和複雜不確定性，各經濟體需要增強自身韌性，也需要更廣泛的全球金融合作。謝啇真指出，過去6年中，全球經濟經歷了多輪供需衝擊，包括新冠疫情、烏克蘭危機引發的歐洲能源供應衝擊、地緣政治危機、人工智慧加速發展帶來的供給不確定性，以及今年以來能源危機帶來的風險。全球經濟需要更強的韌性和敏捷性，以應對能源價格、利率、匯率和通脹等方面的波動。在談及當前市場風險時，謝啇真特別提到，全球股權投資估值高度依賴人工智慧，但AI相關營收回報仍具有很強不確定性，能源和晶片成本也在不斷攀升。AI短期內的快速應用，可能帶來經濟效益不足、安全保障不足等風險。（澎湃）

【分析師：沃什首秀在即 市場緊盯美聯儲新任主席將發出何種訊號】美聯儲將於本週三在新任主席凱文·沃什的領導下，首次宣佈利率決議。屆時他說了什麼、又沒說什麼，都將給市場提供線索，讓外界窺見他承諾要改變的美聯儲溝通方式。市場觀察人士將密切關注會後宣告的措辭變化，以及新主席在風格上是否有任何調整。同時，他們也想知道沃什是否打算繼續保留會後新聞釋出會。此外，外界預計沃什將推動他此前承諾的多項改革，其中包括縮減美聯儲的資產負債表，讓美聯儲重新聚焦貨幣政策。《格蘭特利率觀察家》主編詹姆斯·格蘭特認為，沃什應該直面美聯儲自身財務狀況中那些最不光彩的部分，並加以清理。同時，他還應該在美聯儲的地下金庫裡存放一些黃金，讓它看起來體面些。美聯儲目前並不持有任何黃金。格蘭特還表示，如果財政部不再為美聯儲的虧損買單，早就經營不下去了，美聯儲實際上已經資不抵債。如果仔細看就會發現，美聯儲在固定收益資產和債券上的虧損，遠遠超過它在股東權益賬面上列示的金額。

Euro Flat as Markets Await US-Iran Deal, Fed Policy Signals — The euro held steady at $1.16 as investors awaited details of the US-Iran peace agreement and the Federal Reserve’s monetary policy outlook. Global sentiment improved on a sharp drop in oil prices, with markets on edge ahead of Friday’s signing of the US-Iran deal. The agreement follows a preliminary accord to end the conflict and reopen the Strait of Hormuz after nearly four months of war. Money markets have scaled back bets on ECB tightening, now pricing in less than 30 basis points of hikes this year, equivalent to just one increase. ECB policymaker Gediminas Šimkus reiterated on Wednesday that upside inflation risks still justify further tightening, expecting at least one more hike to keep inflation expectations in check. Later today, attention will shift to the Fed’s policy decision, with focus on updated FOMC projections and new Chair Kevin Warsh’s commentary on the interest rate outlook.

### 2026-06-17 16:24:12  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #DXY #Impact bullish #US10Y #Impact bearish #XAUUSD

【Analysts: Warsh's debut this week; markets watch for signals from new Fed chair】The Federal Reserve's rate decision due Wednesday will be the first under new chair Kevin Warsh. Markets will parse what Warsh says and omits for clues to changes in Fed communication, focusing on the wording of the post-meeting statement, any stylistic shifts and whether the post-decision press conference will be retained. Warsh is expected to push reforms he has outlined, including shrinking the Fed's balance sheet to refocus the central bank on monetary policy. James Grant, editor of Grant's Interest Rate Observer, said Warsh should confront the Fed's weakest financial positions and consider holding gold to improve optics — the Fed currently holds no gold — and warned that if the Treasury stopped covering Fed losses the central bank would have been insolvent, with losses on fixed-income assets and bonds exceeding reported equity.

### 2026-06-17 16:22:48  #Trading Economics #Economy #Georgia #Interest Rate #Importance 1

National Bank of Georgia Keeps Key Rate Unchanged — The National Bank of Georgia held its key policy rate steady at 8.25% during its June 2026 meeting. Headline inflation increased to 5.7% in May, significantly above the central bank’s target of 3%, primarily driven by higher energy prices and ongoing supply chain disruptions. Inflation is expected to remain elevated during the second quarter of the year, averaging 4.9% in 2026. Economic activity remains robust, expanding by 6.2% in April and averaging 8.3% growth overall. However, global uncertainty continues to pose risks to the outlook. An escalation of geopolitical tensions would likely drive commodity prices higher, keeping inflation above target for a longer period. Conversely, stable commodity prices contribute to a faster moderation of inflationary pressures. Future decisions will regard current geopolitical tensions, continuing to moderate in case prolonged conflict and normalization may proceed if tensions ease.

### 2026-06-17 16:21:56  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

German Bund Yields Slide to Three-Month Lows — Germany’s 10-year Bund yields fell toward 2.9%, their lowest since March 17, as traders scaled back expectations for further European Central Bank rate hikes amid a continued decline in oil prices, driven by growing expectations of a US-Iran deal this week. Crude has tumbled to fresh three-month lows, though it remains above pre-conflict levels of around $65 per barrel, with oil flows still far from normal. Money markets now price in less than 30 basis points of ECB tightening this year, equivalent to just one rate hike. ECB policymaker Gediminas Šimkus stated on Wednesday that upside risks to inflation still justify further tightening, and he expects at least one more hike to anchor inflation expectations.

### 2026-06-17 16:18:21  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

South Africa Inflation Rate Quickens to Near 2-Year High — South Africa's annual inflation rate rose for the third month to 4.5% in May 2026, marking the steepest since July 2024, though below the expected 4.7%. The main upward pressure came from prices of transportation (9.4% vs 4.9% in April) and housing & utilities (5.3% vs 5.2%). The core inflation rate, which excludes prices of food, non-alcoholic beverages, fuel, and energy, rose to an over 1-1/2-year high of 3.8% in May, up from 3.6% in the previous month. Monthly, the CPI rose by 0.7% in May, down from a 1.1% increase in the prior month.

【IEA monthly report: Gulf oil supply set for strong rebound but restoration will take months】The IEA says Gulf oil output could rebound to about 8.0 mln b/d by 2027, but full normalization of flows through the Strait of Hormuz will take months as sea mines are cleared and supply chains recover. The agency called this week’s US‑Iran temporary agreement the most significant negotiating breakthrough since the war, but said key details remain unresolved. The IEA now sees global oil demand falling 1.1 mln b/d this year (previous forecast -0.42 mln b/d) on high prices and severe supply disruption, and expects demand to rebound by 2.0 mln b/d next year as trade flows normalize, oil prices ease and the economic outlook improves.

### 2026-06-17 16:14:21  #公司 #觀點

【中信建投董事長劉成：投研、投資、投行、投顧“四投聯動”賦能未來產業發展】在2026北京CBD國際金融論壇暨中信全球財富與投資大會上，中信建投證券董事長劉成表示，未來產業是大國博弈的主戰場。未來產業前景廣闊，但培育週期長、不確定性高。從各方參與者視角審視，至少存在四種結構性痛點：企業有產業鏈但缺抓手、院校有技術但缺場景、政府賽道佈局熱但精準施策難、機構投早投小與退出的矛盾。上述痛點相互交織，構成了未來產業發展的現實痛點。對證券公司而言，破解之道不能靠單一業務條線各自為戰，必須依靠投研、投資、投行、投顧的協同聯動。要以投研為大腦，以投資為紐帶，以投行為抓手，以投顧為延伸，形成合力，形成閉環，才能有效服務未來產業發展。

【國際能源署：2027 年原油供應日增量將回升至 800 萬桶】國際能源署表示，在霍爾木茲海峽原油運輸逐步恢復常態前，海灣地區石油供應衝擊將大幅壓低全球石油需求；機構預計 2027 年原油供應日增量將回升至 800 萬桶。這家總部位於巴黎的能源監管機構稱，本週即將簽署的美伊臨時協議是衝突爆發以來雙方談判取得的最重大突破，但這條關鍵航道的原油運輸完全恢復預計仍需數月時間。該機構代表西方國家及其盟友，受高油價與大規模供應中斷影響，當前下調全年全球石油需求預期：今年全球原油日需求將減少 110 萬桶，此前預測僅減少 42 萬桶。隨著原油貿易流通迴歸正常、油價下行、經濟前景好轉，機構預計明年原油需求日增量將反彈至 200 萬桶。國際能源署在週三釋出的最新月度報告中表示：“儘管協議細節仍有待釐清，多項分歧尚未解決，但本次磋商已是振奮人心的進展。不過運輸完全復甦不會一蹴而就，主航道水雷需要清理，整條供應鏈的修復也需要時間。”伊朗原油出口受美國封鎖衝擊最為嚴重，日出口量銳減 140 萬桶，僅剩餘 23 萬桶 / 日。阿曼灣船對船過駁運輸一定程度彌補出口缺口，該運輸方式常用來掩蓋貨物來源，5 月轉運量大幅攀升，6 月初最高達到 180 萬桶 / 日。

### 2026-06-17 16:07:54  #市場

【上交所釋出《上海證券交易所主動管理交易型開放式證券投資基金業務指引》】6月17日，中國證監會主席吳清在陸家嘴論壇宣佈支援在交易所推出主動ETF。為了保障主動ETF平穩推出，保護投資者合法權益，上交所同日釋出了《上海證券交易所主動管理交易型開放式證券投資基金業務指引》（以下簡稱《指引》）。主動ETF融合了主動管理的專業投研能力與ETF的標準化運作優勢，具備持倉透明、交易便捷等特點。發展主動ETF適應我國資本市場高質量發展階段，有助於服務居民財富管理需求和賦能公募基金高質量發展，對於增強權益投資吸引力，強化資本市場功能升級，吸引中長期資金入市等具有積極意義，是貫徹落實“十五五”規劃建議關於“提高資本市場制度包容性、適應性，健全投資和融資相協調的資本市場功能”，加快上海國際金融中心建設部署、提升上海市場國際競爭力的重要舉措。《指引》共十五條，圍繞主動ETF的特點，主要對主動ETF命名規則、管理人及基金經理資質、產品投資運作、資訊披露、風險防範等方面進行規範。主要內容為：一是明確主動ETF定義和命名規則。一方面，主動ETF是指基金管理人自主選擇投資策略、不以跟蹤特定指數為投資目標並在交易所上市交易的開放式基金；另一方面，基金名稱應當包含“主動管理交易型開放式證券投資基金”字樣，場內簡稱應當包含“主動”字樣。二是明確基金管理人資質要求。基金管理人應當符合公司治理健全、穩定，公司運作合規穩健，主動管理經驗較為豐富等條件。公司應當具備健全有效的投資運作與風險管理制度、充足的投研人員、穩定的ETF運作團隊與技術系統。基金管理人首次開發主動ETF的，應當透過交易所組織的專項檢查驗收。三是明確基金經理資質要求。基金經理應當具備豐富的投資研究經驗，中長期業績良好，投資風格穩定。四是明確產品投資管理要求。基金投資組合應當分佈廣泛且流動性良好，投資策略市場容量充足；應當合理控制基金組合換手率，設定合適的業績比較基準並保持投資風格穩定。五是明確資訊披露相關要求。一方面，基金管理人應當基於真實投資組合製作PCF清單每日披露，並自行或委託相關機構進行IOPV的計算與釋出；另一方面，基金管理人應當在基金資訊披露檔案中做好風險揭示。六是明確風險監測處置相關要求。基金管理人應當建立風險監控系統，並對主動ETF潛在風險進行防範，交易所可對產品運作風險管理情況、合規情況進行檢查。

### 2026-06-17 16:07:07  #央行 #宏觀 #市場

【央行改革政策集中落地！紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）近20個交易日吸金24億 機構：攻守相濟把握結構性機會】6月17日，市場低開高走，上證指數漲0.40%，深證成指漲1.31%，創業板指漲1.56%。在此背景下，紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）下跌0.44%，報1.140元，換手率2.52%，成交額8.25億元，居同類標的ETF首位。資金流向上，紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）長期受資金青睞。近20個交易日資金淨流入24.5億元，近60個交易日資金淨流入31.4億元。截止2026年6月16日，該ETF流通規模329.54億元。 訊息面上，2026陸家嘴論壇重磅政策集中落地，中國人民銀行行長潘功勝一次性官宣了即將出臺的一攬子金融改革舉措，覆蓋短端利率調控最佳化、境外央行人民幣回購工具創設、上海自貿區離岸人民幣外匯交易擴容、特定情景非銀流動性工具研究、釋出《上海國際金融中心發展離岸金融行動方案》等核心內容。整套政策兼顧貨幣政策框架轉型、人民幣國際化推進、金融市場穩定與上海離岸金融建設多重目標。東莞證券認為，下半年市場雖有短期反覆，但中長期向上趨勢未改，宜以攻守平衡思路把握結構性機會。浙商策略表示，配置方面，基於“科技成長調整權重指數回升，新倉待右腳、風格稍均衡”的判斷，行業方面，目前雙創仍舊是市場關注焦點，依舊應作為主線持有，但考慮到近期波動較大、穩健風格受到市場青睞，可以適當切換部分至紅利和大金融板塊。投資者可將紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）作為底倉配置，無股票賬戶的投資者也可透過其場外聯接基金進行配置（A類：007466；C類：007467；I類：022678；Y類：022951）。

### 2026-06-17 16:02:04  #市場

【深交所釋出主動管理ETF業務指引 推動權益類基金產品創新發展】深交所釋出主動管理ETF業務指引，一是規範主動ETF定義與產品命名，要求基金名稱與場內簡稱均需體現“主動”屬性。二是規定基金管理人和基金經理相關要求。基金管理人應當治理健全穩定、合規運作，具備豐富的主動管理經驗，以及健全有效的投資運作與風險管理制度、充足的投研人員、穩定的ETF運作團隊與技術系統等。基金經理應當具備豐富的證券投研經驗，中長期業績良好，投資風格穩定等。基金管理人首次開發主動ETF的，應當透過本所組織的專項檢查驗收。三是明確基金投資管理要求，包括投資組合分佈廣泛且流動性良好，投資策略市場容量充足，合理控制組合換手率，設定合適的業績比較基準並保持投資風格穩定等。四是明確資訊披露與風險防控要求，要求基金管理人充分披露申購贖回清單製作與指數ETF的差異，做好主動ETF的特有風險提示，建立風險監控系統，監控主動ETF執行情況，並對潛在風險進行防範、控制和處理。五是明確主動ETF基金份額的認購、交易和申購贖回，按產品類別適用《深圳證券交易所證券投資基金交易和申購贖回實施細則》等有關規定。

【瑞銀：代理式AI將推動半導體和硬體進一步上行】瑞銀日前釋出亞太科技策略研究報告。該報告就“代理式AI是2026年的關鍵拐點”“儲存器半導體上升週期是否持續”以及“重新審視半導體裝置”等話題進行了探討。瑞銀認為，代理式AI正在加速發展，並被日益視為AI採用過程中的重大拐點。這導致CPU需求顯著提速，原因是需要順序計算週期來訪問多個資源，隨後進行輸出編排和優先順序排序，進而推動獨立通用伺服器以及AI伺服器中 CPU主節點的增長。預計到2027年，主節點CPU佔比將從2025年的29%提升41%。相對於儲存器板塊，晶圓前端裝置股票有望補漲。理由是AI繼續推動DRAM資本開支上行，中國市場和NAND儲存器領域呈增長勢頭，另外，裝置交付週期應在2027年年中趨於正常化，從而在一定程度上緩解產能部署的瓶頸問題。

Indonesia Shares Retreat Ahead of MSCI Reviews — Indonesian equities retreated 55 points or 0.9% to 6,198 on Wednesday afternoon, snapping a three-session rebound as energy, industry, transport, and financials weighed. Early gains faded with sentiment turning cautious ahead of MSCI’s twin reviews: its June 18 Global Market Accessibility Review and June 23 Annual Market Classification Review, both seen as pivotal for Indonesia’s standing in global benchmarks. Fitch Ratings, meanwhile, flagged rising credit risks for local commodity exporters under the centralized export regime. Traders also braced for Thursday’s central bank decision, with policymakers expected to stay hawkish after 75bps of hikes since May to shore up the rupiah and tame inflation, though higher borrowing costs risk curbing demand. In the U.S., markets awaited the first policy move under new Fed chair Kevin Warsh later today. Key laggards included Petrindo Jaya Kreasi (-6.0%), Alamtri Minerals (-3.9%), Indo Tambangraya Megah (-3.4%), and Bank Tabungan Negara (-2.7%).

### 2026-06-17 15:37:45  #Trading Economics #Economy #India #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

India 10Y Yield Falls to 2-Month Low — The yield on India’s 10-Year G-Sec hovered around 6.8%, extending its decline to a two-month low as falling crude oil prices eased concerns over inflation and India's external balances. Brent crude fell 5% for a second consecutive session, taking its cumulative decline over four sessions to around 15%, after details emerged of an interim US-Iran peace agreement that would allow Iran to resume oil sales, extend a ceasefire announced in April by another 60 days. The decline in yields was further reinforced by strong foreign demand for domestic debt, with overseas investors purchasing more than $2 billion worth of Indian bonds over the last eight sessions following the RBI's June 5 measures to boost capital inflows. However, the move lower was limited by profit-taking after the recent rally and caution ahead of the Federal Reserve's policy decision, as investors await guidance from Chair Kevin Warsh on the US interest-rate outlook for 2026.

### 2026-06-17 15:31:59  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

UK Gilt Yields Fall as Inflation Data Eases BoE Pressure — UK 10-year gilt yields dipped toward 4.75%, hitting their lowest since mid-April, as traders scaled back expectations for Bank of England rate hikes following weaker-than-expected UK inflation data and a continued drop in oil prices. Annual CPI held steady at 2.8% in May, falling short of economists’ forecasts for a rise to 3%. However, the services sector saw a faster increase, climbing to 3.7% from 3.2% in April and surpassing the 3.6% estimate. The core inflation rate rose less than expected, edging up to 2.6% from 2.5%. Oil prices, meanwhile, reached fresh three-month lows amid growing expectations of a US-Iran deal this week. Markets now anticipate just 25 basis points of rate increases for 2026, equivalent to a single hike by December. Before the conflict in Iran, the BoE had been expected to cut rates this year. However, the surge in crude prices, still well above pre-war levels of $65 per barrel, disrupted those plans, exposing the UK’s susceptibility to energy shocks.

### 2026-06-17 15:28:15  #Trading Economics #Economy #Kazakhstan #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Kazakhstan Industrial Output Growth Slows in May — Kazakhstan’s industrial production rose by 4.7% year-on-year in May 2026, slowing from a 9.6% advance in the previous month. The slowdown was mainly driven by weaker growth in manufacturing activity (5.2% vs 14.1% in April), water supply, waste management, and pollution control (2.9% vs 8.4%), and mining and quarrying (3.0% vs 9.8%). In contrast, electricity, gas, steam, hot water, and air conditioning supply accelerated sharply (16.0% vs 9.1%). On a monthly basis, industrial output growth picked up to 9.0% in May from 7.2% in the previous month. For the January–May period, industrial production grew by 2.5% compared with the same period a year earlier.

FTSE 100 Treads Water After Inflation Report — The FTSE 100 traded close to flat on Wednesday as investors digested UK inflation data and awaited the Federal Reserve’s policy decision for further signals on the global interest rate outlook. Falling oil prices continued to weigh on the index, with energy majors Shell and BP both down around 1%. BAT and Rio Tinto also declined more than 1%, while Unilever slipped about 0.7%. On the upside, Rolls-Royce gained around 1.5% and AstraZeneca also traded higher. UK markets scaled back expectations for Bank of England rate increases this year after inflation unexpectedly remained unchanged in May, supporting a more cautious approach from some policymakers ahead of Thursday’s meeting. Meanwhile, Brent fell to a three-month low after dropping 15% over four sessions on expectations that a US-Iran agreement to reopen the Strait of Hormuz could boost supply and reduce inflation pressures. Investors are now turning their focus to the Federal Reserve’s latest decision and its view on future policy.

Austria Inflation Rate Confirmed at 3.7% — The annual inflation rate in Austria rose 3.7% in May 2026 from 3.4% in the previous month, matching preliminary estimates. This marked the highest reading since November 2025, driven by faster price growth in transportation (8.8% vs. 7.7% in April) and miscellaneous goods and services (4.8% vs. 3.9%). Meanwhile, inflation remained steady in housing and utilities (3.3%), recreation and culture (1.0%), education (5.4%), and insurance and financial services (4.2%). Additionally, deflation eased in information and communication (-1.0% vs. -2.3%). On the other hand, price growth moderated in food and non-alcoholic beverages (2.2% vs. 2.4%), alcoholic beverages and tobacco (2.0% vs. 3.5%), health (4.4% vs. 4.5%), and restaurants and hotels (4.7% vs. 4.9%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices increased 0.1% in May, slowing from a 0.4% rise in the previous month. The harmonised inflation rate (HICP) also accelerated to 3.7% year-on-year from 3.4% in April.

【Hikvision to raise HDD prices from July 1 as storage costs climb; launches "Guanlan encoding" to cut storage over 50% vs H.265】Market sources said Hikvision has sent price-adjustment letters to distributors, with HDD prices to rise effective July 1. Hikvision said global AI compute buildout and rising data storage demand have pushed some storage-product costs higher in Q3; it will monitor market conditions and seek to ensure stable supply and protect customers. To offset HDD price pressure, Hikvision launched video encoding "Guanlan encoding", driven by the Guanlan large model, using AI-enabled precision encoding to cut video bitrate while preserving image quality and AI application performance. Hikvision said "Guanlan encoding" reduces storage needs by more than 50% versus H.265 and can save up to 90% in some scenarios, aiming to lower customers' storage use and overall build costs.

ANZ: Woshe appears intent on reforms; the press conference may outline reform ideas, with fuller detail likely at the Jackson Hole opening speech in August. BofA: Expects a dovish tone — Woshe may argue the Iran conflict only causes a one-off effect on price levels and should be ‘ignored’ by the Fed, especially after reports of potential resolutions. Capital Economics: Markets risk a more hawkish-than-expected delivery, whether from communication error or because his current stance is less dovish than when courting Trump’s nomination. Yale: Warns that overreliance on soft arguments like an ‘AI disinflation’ thesis while sidelining hard data risks repeating a ‘transitory inflation’ mistake. Nordea: Expects a neutral-to-slightly-hawkish posture to build credibility; any communication shift would be signaling rather than immediate policy action. BNY Mellon: Woshe has criticized forward guidance and may use the press conference — or limit future appearances — to signal changes to Fed communication under his tenure. MFS: Given his views on tech-driven productivity, dovish comments are possible but unlikely, since they would undermine a hawkish image.

### 2026-06-17 15:14:17  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China #CSI300 #Impact bullish

【Report: China asset management industry likely to sustain >5% annual growth over next five years】China Everbright Bank and Everbright Wealth in Beijing released a China Asset Management Market 2024–2025 report saying the industry could sustain annual growth above 5% over the next five years, driven by rising total household wealth, a larger share of financial assets, greater wealth-management penetration and valuation support. Households are reallocating wealth away from property and deposits toward diversified financial assets, following a gradual pathway: fixed income as the base, progressive equity allocation and multi-strategy expansion. Against a backdrop of low deposit rates, retail demand remains focused on principal protection, keeping bank wealth-management and other fixed-income products as the primary safety cushion and main vehicle for flows. Expansion of personal pension schemes and a more developed ETF ecosystem should shift equity allocation from a niche of aggressive investors to marginal inflows from high-net-worth clients and long-duration institutional capital.

### 2026-06-17 15:14:16  #其他

【《中國資產管理市場2015-2026》今日正式釋出：中國資管行業在未來五年或將持續保持5%以上的年增長率】中國光大銀行、光大理財在北京正式釋出《中國資產管理市場2024—2025》報告。報告顯示，在居民總財富、金融資產佔比、財富管理滲透率及估值因子四大因素驅動下，我國資管行業未來五年或將持續保持5%以上的年增長率。報告同時指出，當前我國居民財富從房地產和存款轉向多元金融資產，正在形成一條“固收為基、權益漸進、多策並舉”的漸進式路徑。在“存款利率1時代”背景下，居民對本金安全的訴求依然壓倒對高風險收益的追逐。因此，以銀行理財為代表的固收類產品憑藉其低波穩健的特性，將繼續充當資金轉移的“安全氣囊”和主要載體。同時，隨著個人養老金制度的擴圍與 ETF 生態的完善，權益類資產的配置將不再僅僅是少數激進者的博弈，而是逐步演變為高淨值客戶與長期資金的“邊際增量”選擇。

### 2026-06-17 15:11:28  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Steel Drops to Two-Month Low — Steel rebar futures declined to around CNY 3,120 per ton, reaching their lowest level in two months as disappointing economic data from China weighed on demand expectations. Crude steel production in the world's largest producer continued to weaken amid a prolonged downturn in the property sector, with May output falling 2.7% year-on-year to 84.35 million tons. Broader economic indicators also pointed to slowing momentum, with fixed-asset investment and consumer spending retreating to levels last seen during the pandemic period. Meanwhile, earlier data showed China's steel exports rose 8.8% in May from the previous month, supported by subdued domestic demand and stronger profit margins for overseas shipments. However, cumulative steel exports in the first five months of the year were still down 8.1% from a year earlier, reflecting rising trade barriers in several major export markets.

### 2026-06-17 15:07:07  #Trading Economics #Markets #United Kingdom #Currency #Importance 1

British Pound Weakens After Softer Inflation — The British pound edged lower to $1.34 as traders digested a softer-than-expected inflation report ahead of the Bank of England’s monetary policy decision on Thursday. UK consumer price inflation unexpectedly held steady at 2.8% in May, below expectations of a rise to 3%. Core inflation increased to 2.6%, slightly under forecasts of 2.7%, while services inflation accelerated to 3.7% from 3.2%, broadly in line with expectations. The data suggested that underlying price pressures were less pronounced than feared, reinforcing the cautious, wait-and-see approach favored by some Bank of England policymakers. The central bank is widely expected to leave its benchmark interest rate unchanged at 3.75% this month as it balances still-elevated inflation against a weakening labor market and an economic contraction in April. Investors expect only a single rate increase this year, with a 25bps rise not fully anticipated until December.

【貴州啟動防汛四級應急響應】貴州省應急管理廳訊息，據氣象預報，6月17日至25日前後貴州省將進入新一輪強降雨集中期，累計雨量普遍在150毫米以上，東部和南部局地250～400毫米，個別地區500～700毫米；最強降水時段出現在18日、22日夜間至23日，個別地區最大日降水量達到250～300毫米，最大小時雨強80-120毫米。此輪降雨雨帶在貴州省持續時間長、累計雨量大，累計雨量或雨強可能突破歷史極值，暴雨致災風險高。加之恰逢端午假期和中考時期，前期持續性強降雨導致土壤含水飽和度高、江河底水高，局地發生滑坡、泥石流等地質災害和山洪、江河洪水、城鄉積澇等次生災害風險高。根據《貴州省防汛抗旱應急預案》，經會商研判，省防汛抗旱指揮部決定，於6月17日15時啟動省級防汛四級應急響應。

【海康威視推出”觀瀾編碼”應對硬碟漲價】海康威視相關人士表示，近期，受全球AI算力建設和資料儲存需求持續增長影響，第三季度部分儲存產品成本出現一定幅度上漲。海康威視將持續關注市場變化，努力保障產品穩定供應和客戶利益。 為應對硬碟價格上漲帶來的挑戰，海康威視持續推進技術創新，推出觀瀾大模型驅動的影片編碼技術“觀瀾編碼”，全面佈局到前後端產品。依託AI精準編碼能力，在保障影象質量和智慧應用效果的前提下，可有效降低影片位元速率，相較於H.265可節約50%以上的儲存空間，部分場景最高可節省90%的儲存空間，顯著降低客戶儲存資源佔用和總體建設成本。 觀瀾編碼利用觀瀾大模型精準提取物件，給予物件和背景不同的量化系統策略，對感興趣區域精准保護；採用H.26X的紋理編碼技術，實現既大幅降低儲存成本，又保證關鍵資訊不丟失，不影響智慧分析，且觀瀾編碼廣泛相容，用H.26X標準解碼，保障編碼還原真實。（第一財經）

【機構預計A股震盪走強，AI尚未泡沫化，A500ETF南方（159352）漲超1%】6月17日，A500ETF南方（159352）交投活躍，截至發稿漲1.19%，成交額27.81億元。長江證券表示，在全球AI資本開支加速和國內企業盈利修復的背景下，預計A股市場震盪走強，AI基建+能源安全仍是主線。配置方向上，關注AI基建+資源品兩條主線。一是鑑於科技資本開支超預期，推薦AI硬體，如光模組、儲存、晶片等方向，同時關注提升算力效率的新技術路線-先進封裝，重點關注封測製造、玻璃基板等；二是鑑於美伊衝突逐步緩解，關注有色、化工、機械、汽車等方向。西部證券認為，本輪牛市需要改變AI和“漲價”順週期對立的“二極體思維”兼顧高波動的AI和高賠率的“漲價”順週期。AI尚未泡沫化，成長投資者需要接受AI算力硬體（通訊裝置/半導體/儲存等）的高波動性；PPI漲價終將傳導到CPI，價值投資者既要把握PPI漲價鏈（煤炭/石油/化工/新能源），也要耐心等待下半年“資產負債表修復”有望修復並驅動的CPI漲價鏈（地產/白酒等）。投資者可借道A500ETF南方（159352）及其聯接基金（A類022434；C類022435）一鍵佈局。

### 2026-06-17 14:52:42  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Iron Ore Sinks to Two-Month Low — Iron ore futures dropped toward CNY 750 per ton, reaching their lowest level in two months as ample supply and weakening demand in China continued to weigh on the market. Crude steel production in China has remained under pressure amid a prolonged downturn in the property sector, with May output declining 2.7% year-on-year to 84.35 million tons. Broader economic indicators also disappointed, with fixed-asset investment and consumer spending retreating to levels not seen since the pandemic period. On the supply side, Guinea’s Simandou mine continued to ramp up production, with output rising to 2.2 million tons in May from 1.3 million tons in April. Meanwhile, iron ore inventories at Chinese ports climbed to record highs as demand from steelmakers softened. Adding to bearish sentiment, China’s iron ore imports fell nearly 6% in May from the previous month, defying expectations for an increase, as mills limited purchases to immediate needs ahead of a seasonally weaker demand period.

ASX 200 Closes at Two-Month High — The ASX 200 rose 49 points, or 0.5%, to finish at 8,966 on Wednesday, extending gains for a fourth session and notching a two-month peak. Sentiment was lifted by firmer U.S. futures ahead of Kevin Warsh’s first Federal Reserve meeting. At the same time, key details emerged on the interim U.S.-Iran agreement, which allows oil tanker passage through Hormuz in exchange for lifting port blockades. Locally, the Reserve Bank of Australia held cash rates at 4.35% Tuesday, in line with estimates, as the board balanced slowing growth against sticky inflation. Most sectors advanced, led by producer manufacturing, consumer services, and non-energy minerals. Index heavyweight BHP Group rose 0.6%, briefly hitting a record high. Meanwhile, gold miners surged as bullion rallied on Middle East news, with Northern Star up 2.5% and Regis 6.1%. Three of the four major banks added between 0.2% and 1.2%. In contrast, Karoon Energy slumped 13.4% after cutting its annual output forecast.

### 2026-06-17 14:41:12  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hungary #Wage Growth #Importance 1

Hungary Gross Wage Growth Eases in April — Hungary’s gross average wages rose 9% year-on-year to HUF 772,200 in April 2026, easing from a 9.2% increase in the previous month. Wage growth in the public sector moderated to 10.7% from 11.8% in March, while earnings growth also slowed in the non-profit sector to 7.6% from 10.1%. In contrast, wage growth in the private sector accelerated to 8.7% from 8.4%. Additionally, gross wages excluding one-off payments and bonuses increased 8.4% year-on-year to HUF 711,400 in April. In the first four months of 2026, average gross wages rose 13.4% from the same period a year earlier, reaching HUF 779,500.

Fed to Keep Rates Steady at New Chair's First Meeting — The Federal Reserve is widely expected to keep the federal funds rate unchanged at 3.50%-3.75% for a fourth consecutive meeting in June 2026, as policymakers navigate persistent inflation and uncertainty over the pace at which price pressures will ease. This meeting will be the first under new Fed Chair Kevin Warsh, whose appointment was initially viewed as leaning toward interest rate cuts and policy easing; however, market expectations have since shifted toward a more hawkish stance. Although the US and Iran have agreed to a provisional peace deal, oil prices remain above pre-war levels, while recent labour market data has pointed to continued strength in employment. Investors will closely watch the Fed’s updated economic projections, including the “dot plot,” for signals on the future path of interest rates. However, many analysts expect that new Chair Warsh will not participate in it. Back in March, the Fed had previously projected one rate cut in 2026 and another one in 2027.

### 2026-06-17 14:33:44  #Trading Economics #Economy #China #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

China 10Y Yield Falls on PBOC Policy Shift Signals — China's 10-year government bond yield fell to 1.73% on Wednesday, hitting a fresh one-week low as investors assessed signals that the People's Bank of China could adjust its monetary policy framework. Speaking at the Lujiazui Forum, PBOC Governor Pan Gongsheng said the central bank would optimize its management of short-term interest rates and expand its overnight reverse repo operations at an appropriate time. The remarks reinforced expectations that the PBOC may gradually transition away from relying on the seven-day reverse repo rate as its main policy anchor, aligning its operating framework more closely with those of major global central banks while enhancing its control over short-term funding conditions. Separately, at the same forum, Chinese Vice Premier He Lifeng said Beijing plans to incorporate anti-sanctions provisions into its financial legislation, aiming to strengthen the country's ability to counter what he described as "unreasonable" external restrictions.

Offshore Yuan Holds Near a Multi-Year High — The offshore yuan traded around 6.75 per dollar on Wednesday, remaining close to its strongest level since February 2023 after the People's Bank of China unveils fresh measures designed to deepen global usage of the Chinese currency. The central bank said it will expand access to yuan liquidity through its Foreign and International Monetary Authorities (FIMA) repo facility, enabling foreign central banks, sovereign wealth funds, and other official institutions to borrow yuan against holdings of Chinese government bonds and other eligible securities. The announcement marks another step in Beijing's long-running campaign to internationalize the yuan and reduce reliance on the US dollar in global finance. Reflecting this ambition, China's latest five-year plan pledged to advance yuan internationalization, while President Xi Jinping has emphasized the objective of building a strong and influential global currency.

### 2026-06-17 14:25:30  #其他

【米哈遊訴多摩萬事屋勝訴】6月17日，米哈遊法務部發文：米哈遊訴“多摩萬事屋”著作權侵權及不正當競爭糾紛一案迎來終審判決。上海智慧財產權法院二審駁回上訴，維持一審法院判決，即判令各被告立即停止侵權、公開發布宣告消除影響，並連帶賠償米哈遊經濟損失及合理開支共計298萬元。 “多摩萬事屋”是一家在全國多地開設連鎖門店的線下商業集合店鋪，其背後涉及多家關聯企業主體。該店鋪打著“同人二創”的旗號，以企業身份進行大規模商業化經營： 1.以直營及加盟方式在全國開設了數十家門店，大量收購同人作品後，貼附自家品牌標識進行二次包裝，再在店內大量銷售未經授權的《原神》《崩壞：星穹鐵道》《未定事件簿》《絕區零》《崩壞3》等周邊產品； 2.經取證，涉案商品涵蓋徽章、立牌、擺件等品類，涉及141個遊戲角色、共計1500餘款商品； 3.對外進一步開展加盟業務，將侵權經營進行規模化擴張。 一審訴訟中，“多摩萬事屋”試圖將侵權責任推卸給二創作者，聲稱“如果是侵權商品，那構成侵權的也是同人畫師或社團”。但米哈遊認為，即使“多摩萬事屋”敗訴，也無權向同人作者追責。 一審法院經審理認定，“多摩萬事屋”的行為構成著作權侵權及不正當競爭。判令各被告停止侵權、消除影響，並連帶賠償298萬元。 一審判決後，“多摩萬事屋”不服並提起上訴，辯稱自身僅為“寄售方”而非生產者。上海智慧財產權法院二審對此予以明確否定，認定其自行選品、主動貼附自有標識並透過特定渠道銷售，已使公眾認為商品來源於該店鋪，“生產者”身份依法成立，應當承擔相應侵權責任。這一認定也避免了侵權責任被惡意轉嫁給二創作者。

3. 巴克萊：最新點陣圖或反映出更高的通脹預期和更謹慎的政策立場，即2026年全年維持利率不變、2027年僅降息一次、2028年繼續按兵不動。
4. 傑富瑞：沃什在參議院聽證會上就明確表示不認同前瞻性指引。這將成為最大變化，具體表現為FOMC宣告篇幅縮短，以及SEP細節減少。
5. 凱投宏觀：預計沃什不會提出自己的利率預測，但在新聞釋出會上仍會被問及他的看法。
6. 摩根大通：預計沃什將會提交其個人預測，否則將顯得如同對他自己所領導的委員會表達出一種立場鮮明的異議。
7. 道明證券：預計沃什將不提交個人點陣圖預測 ，以此作為策略性舉措，最大限度淡化6月點陣圖可能釋放的鷹派訊號。
8. 美國銀行：預計沃什將不提交個人預測，因其不相信前瞻指引。經濟增長預測或將下調至2.1%，通脹將被明顯上調，失業率預測可能小幅下調或維持不變。
9. 荷蘭合作銀行：預計風險偏向於通脹更頑固、降息更少甚至進行加息，而不是迅速好轉。樂觀預期已經落空。
10. 北歐聯合銀行：預計點陣圖將不會再包含3月份時曾預期的年內降息情境，甚至可能逐出現一些加息的呼聲。

### 2026-06-17 14:16:30  #MKT News #Important #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #DXY #Impact bullish #US10Y #S&P500 #Impact bearish

【Preview: institutions expect Fed SEP/dot plot to tilt hawkish — Waller’s personal dot-plot status key】UBS, Goldman, Barclays, PIMCO and other institutions expect the Fed’s SEP/dot plot to show higher inflation and a more cautious policy path, pushing rate cuts later or reducing the number of cuts implied earlier this year. UBS: expects inflation forecasts to be revised up; most FOMC members see no rate cuts before 2028, though the median could still show a single 2028 cut while overall policy remains restrictive. Goldman: says Waller may not submit a personal dot plot; expects the median to show rates unchanged through 2026 with one cut in 2027 and another in 2028, and anticipates 2026 GDP and unemployment slightly lower but inflation materially higher. Barclays: expects a hawkish tilt — rates held all of 2026, only one cut in 2027, and no easing in 2028. Jefferies: flags Waller’s rejection of forward guidance as likely, implying a shorter FOMC statement and reduced SEP detail. Capital Economics: expects Waller will decline to provide a personal policy path but will still be pressed on his views at the press conference. JP Morgan: expects Waller to submit a personal forecast; not doing so would signal clear dissent from his committee leadership. TD Securities and BofA: anticipate Waller will not submit a personal dot plot; BofA expects growth forecasts to be trimmed to ~2.1% while inflation is revised up and unemployment is unchanged or slightly lower. Rabobank: sees risk skewed toward stickier inflation, fewer cuts or even upside to rates rather than a quick improvement. Nordea: expects removal of the intra-year cut scenario priced in March and even some calls for hikes. BNY Mellon: expects a modestly hawkish adjustment, likely dropping the previous expectation of a 2026 cut. PIMCO: expects a significant hawkish shift in the dot plot; several committee members may show projections for higher 2026 rates even if the median remains unchanged.

### 2026-06-17 14:08:46  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Inflation Rate #Importance 2

UK Inflation Surprisingly Holds at 2.8% — The annual inflation rate in the UK stood at 2.8% in May 2026, unchanged from the previous month and below market expectations of 3.0%. The reading remained at its lowest level since March last year. Inflation slowed in housing and household services (2.7% vs. 3.0% in April), the softest level in almost two years, as owner-occupiers’ housing costs continued to ease, while food and non-alcoholic beverages decelerated further (2.2% vs. 3.0%), hitting their lowest level since December 2024. Price growth also moderated in clothing and footwear (0.2% vs. 0.7%) and recreation and culture (1.5% vs. 1.7%). This was offset by upward pressure from transport inflation, which accelerated sharply to 6.8%, the highest since December 2022, up from 4.5% in April, driven by higher motor fuel prices, rising air fares, and an upward effect from vehicle excise duty (VED). On a monthly basis, the CPI rose by 0.2% in May, below forecasts of a 0.4% gain and easing from a 0.7% increase in April.

UK Core Inflation Slightly Below Estimates — The UK's annual core inflation rate edged up to 2.6% in May 2026 from April's nearly five-year low of 2.5%, though it came in slightly below market expectations of 2.7%. Core goods inflation eased to 2.0% from 2.4%, while core services inflation accelerated to 3.7% from 3.2%. On a monthly basis, core consumer prices rose 0.3%, slowing from a 0.7% increase in April and falling short of forecasts of 0.4%. It also marked the softest monthly rise since January.

【UK May CPI unexpectedly unchanged at 2.8%】UK May CPI held at 2.8% YoY, matching April and below economists' 3.0% forecast, in data released Wednesday one day before the Bank of England's policy decision. Inflation remains roughly 1 percentage point above the BoE's January projection; in April the Bank warned headline CPI could exceed 3.5% by year-end and, in the three worst-case scenarios, top 6% in early next year. Markets were relieved this week by an apparent US-Iran agreement that could reopen the Strait of Hormuz; the UK, as a major importer of gas, is more exposed to the conflict than most Western peers. Economists expect the BoE MPC to hold Bank Rate at 3.75% this month by a 7-2 vote.

### 2026-06-17 14:06:54  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

UK Producer Inflation Matches Forecasts in May — Factory gate prices for UK-manufactured goods advanced 4.0% year-on-year in May 2026, in line with market expectations but slightly easing from an upwardly revised 4.1% rise in April, which was the highest reading since May 2023. Almost all 10 product groups made upward contributions to the annual rate, led by a surge in prices of coke and refined petroleum products (55.6% vs 51.1% in April) amid higher energy costs. Inflation also accelerated for other outputs from manufacturing (4.3% vs 3.1% in April) and basic metals, fabricated metal products and machinery (4.2% vs 4.1%). Meanwhile, motor vehicles and other transport equipment provided the largest downward contribution, with prices falling 1.7% (vs 2.2% in April). The decline was due to supply chain issues and other market factors. Monthly, factory gate prices rose 0.5%, slowing from an upwardly revised 1.5% increase in April, matching market forecasts and marking the softest increase since February, when producer prices fell.

### 2026-06-17 14:05:41  #市場

【中國證監會：支援港股上市公司境內上市 推進人民幣外匯期貨試點】今天，2026年陸家嘴論壇在上海開幕，本屆論壇主題為“全球治理倡議下的金融發展與合作：新願景、新挑戰和新機遇”。在本次論壇上，中國證監會主席吳清提出多項舉措。

鼓勵外資市場機構參與基金投顧等業務

具體舉措包括：支援符合條件的港股上市公司境內上市，更好促進兩地市場聯動發展；加快研究推進人民幣外匯期貨試點，以應對匯率波動風險、支援企業國際化發展；支援香港於近期啟動5年期人民幣國債期貨上市交易，不斷提升外資長期配置中國資產的便利化水平，以及支援外資金融機構來華展業等。

吳清表示，還應積極支援外資在華新設投資或者合資的證券、基金、期貨機構，鼓勵外資市場機構參與基金投顧，擴大試點等等，更好促進在華展業興業。

大力支援上市公司併購及再融資

吳清表示，將進一步激發併購重組活力，用好用足併購重組快速稽核機制，積極打通併購實施過程中的堵點卡點，推動最佳化併購相關政策，降低交易成本。

今年在二月初，滬深北交易所都發布實施了最佳化再融資的一攬子措施。突出扶優扶科的導向，一些優質上市公司高效實現融資稽核用時。將抓緊修訂《上市公司證券發行政策的管理辦法》等制度規則，加快推出儲架發行等機制，進一步提升公司再融資的靈活性、便利度。

將持續豐富資本市場各類投資產品和工具

其中包括：支援在滬深交易所推出主動ETF；推出商業不動產REITs試點，4單首批專案18日將在上交所掛牌上市；推出支援中小基金公司規範健康發展的一攬子措施，支援中小基金公司差異化發展。

吳清表示，包括堅持分類監管，突出特色，在產品佈局與業務准入等方面給予適當的傾斜。積極支援中小基金的差異化發展。持續提升監管效能和適應性。在增強制度包容性，提高市場含客量的同時，還將不斷提升監管科技化、智慧化的水平。

依法打擊各類跨境違法違規經營行為

持牌經營是金融監管的基本要求，也是對金融產品消費者有效保護的基本要求。中國證監會將進一步推動健全跨境監管協調機制，切實維護市場正常秩序。

近期，中國證監會會同有關部門堅決查處了個別機構違法違規的跨境展業問題，受到國內國際高度關注。

吳清表示，將堅持開正門、堵偏門，既支援合法合規的跨境投融資活動，又依法打擊各類跨境違法行為。（央視新聞）

### 2026-06-17 14:00:20  #Trading Economics #Economy #Kyrgyzstan #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Kyrgyzstan Inflation Eases Slightly in May — The annual inflation rate in Kyrgyzstan eased slightly to 11.0% in May 2026 from 11.4% in the previous month, which had marked a near three-year high. Price pressures moderated in food and non-alcoholic beverages (9.8% vs 10.8% in April), housing and utilities (9.7% vs 12.7%), healthcare (11.4% vs 12.0%), recreation and culture (11.5% vs 11.7%), education (22.3% vs 22.4%), and miscellaneous goods and services (20.9% vs 20.4%). Meanwhile, inflation accelerated for alcoholic beverages and tobacco (11.7% vs 10.9%), clothing and footwear (7.1% vs 6.5%), household goods and appliances (9.3% vs 8.6%), and transport (11.8% vs 10.2%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices edged up 0.8% in May, following a 0.7% increase in the previous month.

【Bank of Korea warns AI-driven semiconductor boom could complicate inflation outlook】The Bank of Korea said on Wednesday an AI-driven boom in the semiconductor sector — marked by exceptionally large bonus payouts at some chipmakers and major tech firms — could complicate the inflation outlook by prompting broader wage demands. If bonus-driven wage gains spill beyond tech, they would lift consumer demand and corporate costs and, together with rising energy prices, add to inflationary pressure. While broad wage acceleration has not yet materialized, the central bank urged close monitoring of outsized bonus payments and rising wage demands as policymakers work to prevent transient supply shocks from becoming persistent inflation.

NZX 50 Closes 0.3% Lower Ahead of GDP Data, Fed Decision — The NZX 50 fell 33 points, or 0.3%, to close at 13,393 on Wednesday, erasing gains from the previous session, as traders remained cautious ahead of New Zealand's Q1 GDP data and the Fed's monetary policy decision. Fresh data also pressured sentiment, as New Zealand's consumer confidence fell to its lowest level since 2023 in Q2, weighed by rising fuel and other living costs, while the Q1 current account gap widened from the same period a year earlier. The broader index declined from its highest level since March 6, reached a day earlier, tracking a fall in Wall Street's Nasdaq and S&P 500 indexes overnight amid a pullback in AI-related stocks. Technology stocks, materials, utilities, financials, and communication services mainly weighed on the index, with notable losses from Delegat Group (-2.6%), Hallenstein Glasson (-2.5%), and Mainfreight (-2.0%), while Ebos Group, Infratil, and Summerset Group each fell 1.3%, and ANZ Group declined 0.9%.

【PBOC governor Pan to narrow temporary overnight repo band, expand overnight reverse repos】At the Lujiazui Forum on June 17, PBOC governor Pan Gongsheng said the central bank will narrow the temporary overnight repo rate corridor from 70bps to a symmetric 50bps and will add overnight reverse-repo instruments as needed. Pan said the PBOC will move the timing of temporary repo operations earlier and disclosed trigger conditions: DR001 persistently below or above the corresponding temporary repo operation rate would activate the tool to better anchor market expectations. The measures are presented as accelerating the shift to a price-based monetary framework to strengthen transmission from policy rates to market rates. DR001 has traded about ±20bps around policy rates this year; the PBOC said specific overnight reverse-repo operational details will be set by market demand and implemented progressively.

### 2026-06-17 13:36:09  #央行 #其他

【專家解讀兩大舉措完善短端利率調控機制】中國人民銀行行長潘功勝6月17日在2026陸家嘴論壇上提出，完善短端利率調控機制的兩項舉措，一是將臨時隔夜正/逆回購的操作利率區間由此前的70bp收窄為對稱的50bp；二是將適時增加公開市場隔夜逆回購操作品種。專家表示，這是持續推動貨幣政策框架向價格型轉型，使政策利率向市場利率傳導不斷增強的重要舉措。今年以來，隔夜利率DR001基本在政策利率上下20bp的區間執行，央行順勢收窄臨時回購利率區間，也體現了穩中求進的總體原則。上述專家表示，央行此次還將臨時回購的操作時間適當提前，並披露了工具的觸發條件，即DR001持續低於或高於臨時回購相應操作利率，能更好穩定市場預期。專家表示，此次央行宣佈將適時增加隔夜逆回購品種，既是順應市場需求，也有利於增強短端利率調控的精準性和有效性。隔夜逆回購的具體操作安排將結合市場需求確定，也體現了循序漸進的改革思路。（中證金牛座）

PBoC Moves to Strengthen Rate Framework — The People's Bank of China announced on Wednesday several measures to improve its short-term interest rate framework, signaling a possible shift toward using the overnight policy rate as its main benchmark instead of the current seven-day reverse repo rate. Governor Pan Gongsheng said the central bank will narrow the interest rate corridor for its temporary overnight repo and reverse repo facilities while increasing overnight reverse repo operations. He said further that the central bank is developing tools to provide emergency liquidity to non-bank financial institutions during times of market stress. Chinese authorities also rolled out new measures to promote the yuan's international use, including a repo facility allowing foreign monetary authorities and sovereign wealth funds to access yuan liquidity using bonds as collateral, alongside a pilot program for offshore yuan foreign exchange trading in the Shanghai Free Trade Zone.

【Institution forecasts for Fed rate path: pause expected; views diverge on timing of cuts and upside risk】Most institutions expect the Fed to hold at the upcoming meeting and to remove dovish forward guidance; views diverge on timing and direction of the next move. MOODY'S: hold; cuts unlikely short-term, baseline no cut this year; could hike if inflation expectations rise. Nomura: hold; near-term cuts unlikely, possibly unchanged through 2026. JP Morgan: hold; rates to remain unchanged for the rest of the year, policy shifting from easing toward neutral. Wells Fargo: hold; would only hike if clear labour-market overheating or worse inflation. BNY MELLON: hold; statement likely to signal two-way risks and drop 2026 cut expectations; no moves this year. Goldman Sachs: hold; expects removal of easing guidance and two 25bp cuts in Jun and Dec 2027. UBS: hold; expects abandonment of dovish wording and 25bp cuts in Mar and Jun 2027. CITIGROUP: hold; expects three 25bp cuts in Sep, Oct and Dec. Commerzbank: hold; may begin cuts mid-next year, cumulative -75bp by end-2027. Capital Economics: hold but sees high probability of two “insurance” hikes in Dec and early next year. BNP Paribas: hold; little chance of pre-midterm hike, earliest hike in Dec, pace milder than 2022. Deutsche Bank: hold; baseline unchanged long-term but upside risk rising. PGIM: hold; expects three hikes this year, then three cuts in 2027 and one in 2028, terminal rate ~3.375%. Barclays, BofA, ANZ and Mitsubishi UFJ: expect hold and likely deletion of dovish phrasing; BofA may adjust employment wording and ANZ expects reaffirmed 2% inflation commitment. MFS: hold; may signal a neutral stance and consider dropping the dot-plot and reducing press-conference time.

【韓國央行預計通脹將持續高於目標水平 直至明年】韓國央行週三表示，儘管美伊結束戰爭的談判取得進展，但由於價格上漲壓力仍然較大，通脹預計將持續高於其目標水平直至明年。韓國央行表示：“展望未來，通脹預計將在相當長的一段時間內保持高位。儘管隨著戰爭局勢的緩和，油價將逐漸下降，但高油價和匯率帶來的成本上漲壓力預計將傳導至其他產品。”韓國央行在其半年一次的通脹目標政策報告中表示，由於工資上漲（例如一些科技公司發放鉅額獎金）帶來的日益增長的需求側壓力，今年下半年消費者通脹率將保持在3%左右，明年將繼續超過央行2%的中期目標。央行的展望進一步強化了最早於下個月收緊貨幣政策的理由。受中東衝突導致油價上漲的影響，5月消費者通脹率升至兩年多來新高3.1%。

South Korean Won Halts Gains — The South Korean won weakened to around 1,515 per dollar, ending its recent advance, as demand for the greenback firmed amid cautious positioning ahead of the Federal Reserve’s policy decision. Market participants focused on potential signals from the FOMC under Fed Chair Kevin Warsh regarding the future policy path, with uncertainty over the outlook keeping FX markets defensive. Broader risk-off sentiment, driven by weakness in global technology stocks and declines in US AI-related shares, supported safe-haven flows into the dollar and weighed on Asian currencies. The won also came under pressure from ongoing portfolio rebalancing flows and softer regional risk appetite. Meanwhile, expectations that the Bank of Korea will maintain a relatively tight policy stance helped limit the pace of losses. Authorities’ continued monitoring of foreign exchange volatility also provided some support, preventing sharper depreciation.

【Qatar reroutes LNG carriers to Middle East as Strait of Hormuz poised to reopen】Following a US-Iran deal that has the Strait of Hormuz poised to reopen, Qatar has begun repositioning LNG carriers to the Middle East to prepare for higher exports. Vessel-tracking shows at least four Qatar-owned empty LNG carriers that were idle or diverted are now turning back toward the region, with Ras Laffan listed as their next port; a Qatar-chartered ship is also en route. Separately, four Qatar-linked tankers are anchored in the Gulf of Oman and may attempt transit through Hormuz into the Persian Gulf. Qatar has not sent empty ships into the Gulf since the February outbreak of war. The moves account for only a small portion of Qatar’s roughly 70-vessel fleet but signal readiness to lift shipments if the strait reopens.

### 2026-06-17 12:23:35  #Trading Economics #Economy #Cape Verde #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Cape Verde Inflation Rate Rises from 2-Year Low — The annual inflation rate in Cape Verde rose to 0.8% in May 2026 from 0.3% in the previous month, which marked the lowest reading since March 2024. Upward pressure came mainly from transportation (3.4% vs 0.8% in April), alcoholic beverages and tobacco (3.9% vs 3.8%), and food and non-alcoholic beverages (0.8% vs 0.2%). Additionally, deflation eased in housing and utilities (-3.8% vs -4.2%). Meanwhile, price growth slowed in furnishings and household equipment (6.2% vs 6.7%), clothing and footwear (4.2% vs 4.7%), health (2.9% vs 3.1%), recreation and culture (1.3% vs 1.5%), education (0.2% vs 0.3%), hotels, restaurants and cafés (3.5% vs 3.9%), and miscellaneous goods and services (0.6% vs 1.3%), while communication costs declined (-0.3% vs 0.1%). On a monthly basis, the CPI rose 0.5%, following gains of 0.1% in each of the previous two months.

【Hong Kong midday: Hang Seng down 0.37%; PCB, newly listed AI names extend three-day rally】By the midday break the Hang Seng Index was down 0.37% after plunging more than 200 points earlier and breaching the 24,400 level. The index opened near 24,495, ran to 24,560 before heavy selling pushed an intraday low of 24,272 (-221 points); losses narrowed to under 100 points before the break. The Hang Seng Tech Index was up 0.17%. Market turnover was HKD 145.51 bln. PCB names and newly listed AI stocks rose for a third session; storage, semiconductor and domestic retail stocks staged a strong rebound. Building materials & cement, electronic components and power equipment led gains. Rare earths, property management and computer hardware pulled back; internet healthcare, autos and oil & gas fell for a third straight day; aluminum and dairy were weak. 【 何立峰出席2026陸家嘴論壇開幕式並講話 】 中共中央政治局委員、國務院副總理何立峰17日出席2026陸家嘴論壇開幕式並講話。中共中央政治局委員、上海市委書記陳吉寧出席活動。何立峰指出，近年來，中國在金融領域堅持防風險強監管促高質量發展的工作主線，推動金融事業不斷向前發展。當前百年變局正在加速演進，世界經濟發展和全球金融執行面臨考驗。中國將錨定中國特色金融發展之路不動搖、不偏航，全面落實“十五五”規劃綱要部署，服務經濟高質量發展，為世界經濟注入穩定力量。中國將繼續穩步擴大金融領域制度型開放，歡迎全球各國金融機構深耕中國市場，共享中國發展機遇。何立峰強調，面對全球發展和治理難題，唯有基於國際規則和多邊主義的相互尊重、和平共處、合作共贏才是正道。中國將不斷完善金融法治，堅定不移維護國家金融安全。中國願與各方一道，加強全球金融治理合作，攜手應對風險挑戰。何立峰指出，上海匯聚全球金融要素資源，作為國際金融中心的獨特優勢不斷顯現，將支援上海先行先試發展離岸金融，加快建設人民幣資產全球配置中心和風險管理中心，提升跨境貿易投資便利化水平，進一步豐富完善包括航運保險在內的信貸、保險、債券等產品體系，推動上海國際金融中心建設實現新的突破和提升。（新華社）

【吳清：對投資者透過合法渠道進行跨境投資給予支援和保護】6月17日，證監會主席吳清在2026陸家嘴論壇上表示，近期國際國內都比較關注有關方面對跨境展業的整治，這裡面也有一些誤讀。吳清談到，近期證監會會同有關方面堅決查處了個別機構的違法違規跨境展業這樣的問題，接下來還會進一步推動健全跨境監管協調機制，強化日常資訊共享和市場溝通。開正門、堵偏門，既支援合法合規的跨境投融資活動，又依法打擊各類跨境違法行為。“沒有在境內持牌的機構在境內開展跨境證券交易服務，這是在任何地方都不能允許的，（整治）是針對這種未持牌機構的違法經營的行為，並不是整治投資者跨境投資。”吳清說。吳清指出，對投資者透過合法渠道的跨境投資，我們還是給予支援，也給予有效保護的。“沒有在境內持牌的機構在境內從事業務，不管是在境外持牌的機構，還是在境內設立的機構，沒持牌都是不能經營的，這是整個金融監管基本的要求，也是對投資者和金融產品消費者有效保護的基本要求。”吳清進一步指出，“所以我們要依法打擊的是這種各類的跨境違法、違規的經營行為，切實維護市場正常秩序，切實有效地保護投資者的合法權益。”

### 2026-06-17 12:07:06  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 2

Brent Extends Decline for Fifth Straight Session — Brent fell toward $78 per barrel on Wednesday, sliding for the fifth straight session and reaching its lowest level since early March, as expectations of increased supply continued to weigh on prices ahead of the signing of a peace agreement between the US and Iran. The two countries are scheduled to sign an interim deal in Switzerland on Friday, offering Tehran broad economic incentives, including the immediate resumption of its oil exports. Tankers from other nations are also expected to resume passage through the Strait of Hormuz once the agreement takes effect, although shipping companies remain cautious about its long-term durability. Additional supplies from the region are expected to replenish refinery inventories globally, alongside higher OPEC+ export quotas and increased output from the UAE, which left the cartel during the conflict. Meanwhile, industry data showed that US crude inventories declined by 8.3 million barrels last week.

Soybeans Hit Two-Week High — Soybean futures climbed above $11.3 per bushel, hitting a two-week high on signs of renewed Chinese import demand, although favorable US crop weather limited further upside. Reports indicate Chinese buyers are looking to secure US cargoes for fourth-quarter shipment, though traders say no firm deals have been confirmed yet. Markets have been closely monitoring Chinese purchasing activity following the May announcement that China would buy $17 billion of US agricultural products annually, alongside an existing commitment of 25 million metric tons of soybeans. Meanwhile, widespread rain and warm temperatures across the US Midwest have generally benefited crop development over the past week, reinforcing expectations of adequate global supply. The USDA also rated 66% of US soybeans in good-to-excellent condition, up one percentage point from a week earlier. A sharp decline in crude oil prices after an outline US-Iran peace deal added further pressure on grain and oilseed markets.

【AI compute demand boosts PMIC supply-chain pricing power】AI server, edge AI and high-performance compute demand is forcing power-architecture upgrades and reviving PMIC sector momentum. Tightening mature-process (8‑inch) foundry capacity and recent wafer-foundry price increases are expected to improve IC/PMIC suppliers' pricing power. Selected PMIC vendors reported mixed Jan–May results, but AI power, DDR5 PMICs and fan-motor driver IC demand should help operations reaccelerate in H2. TSMC and Samsung are reconfiguring 8‑inch capacity; limited global 8‑inch supply growth plus rising demand for AI power-management ICs, power components, display drivers and automotive/industrial control is lifting utilization and has prompted some fabs to raise 8‑inch foundry fees, supporting a shift from PMIC cyclical trough toward potential structural recovery.

【我國將制定統一的航空醫療救護標準】 記者今天瞭解到，市場監管總局（國家標準委）近日下達國家標準制定計劃，由北京市地方標準《航空醫療救護服務規範》轉化升級的《應急救援 航空醫療救護通用要求》國家標準工作啟動。長期以來，我國航空醫療救護行業缺乏統一的技術規範，運營主體參差不齊、服務流程要求不一、權責邊界劃分不清、裝備配置要求不明，嚴重製約了航空醫療體系整體效能的發揮。此次擬修訂的國家標準，將全面吸納北京地方標準的核心內容與實踐經驗，緊扣資源完備、流程規範、管理有效、持續改進四項工作重點，從機構資質、人員配置、裝備設施、服務流程、安全管理等方面作出系統性規定，適用於各類有人駕駛航空器開展的醫療救護工作。《應急救援 航空醫療救護通用要求》國家標準的統一制定，將有力推動全國立體化航空醫療救護體系的構建，打通全域覆蓋、響應迅速的“空中生命線”，夯實我國應急救援體系建設基礎。（央視新聞）

【CITIC Securities H2 Outlook: AI and Energy/Chemicals Form a New Barbell】CITIC Securities says global economic resilience is being tested by the Middle East conflict; H2 will be dominated by coexisting supply shocks, policy constraints and investment support, with the persistence of oil-price shocks the primary variable for the global macro path. For China, policymakers are likely to shift H2 focus toward structural reform—curbing domestic over-competition (anti-involution) and enforcing carbon assessments to optimize supply, while using fiscal and tax reform to steer demand. In China’s A-share market, AI plus energy/chemicals form a new barbell. Investment stance: keep a global lens on China and adopt K-shaped positioning rather than simple bull/bear views; move from “building AI” to “adapting to AI” in technology exposure. The long-term investment case remains a re-rating of China’s advantaged manufacturing pricing power; priority sectors are new energy, chemicals, nonferrous metals and power equipment. On the offensive side, monitor domestic AI progress and overweight the domestic compute chain and cloud platforms. Continue selective overweight of low-valuation names, notably brokers and insurers.

【我國沙化土地面積年均縮減1000萬畝】記者從國家林草局獲悉，我國沙化土地面積持續淨減少，由上世紀末的年均擴充套件515萬畝轉變為目前的年均縮減1000萬畝。資料顯示，八大沙漠、四大沙地的土壤風蝕總量較2000年下降約40%，沙區植被平均蓋度達21.17%，較10年前上升2.84%。“三北”工程區林草覆蓋率達40.76%，67.82%的可治理沙化土地得到治理。據介紹，近年來我國以打好打贏“三北”工程攻堅戰為重點，全面推進荒漠化防治。“三北”工程三大標誌性戰役打響以來，累計下達中央投資889億元，組織實施重點專案544個，完成建設任務2.44億畝。“十五五”是縱深推進全國防沙治沙和打好“三北”工程攻堅戰的攻堅期和決勝期。下一步，各地各部門將緊扣規劃目標任務，堅持分類保護、分割槽治理，推進重點區域沙化土地綜合治理，適度發展綠色沙產業。力爭到2030年，全國完成沙化土地治理任務近1億畝，四大沙地、沙漠綠洲、青藏高原、黃河流域、京津冀周邊等重點區域生態狀況顯著改善。（新華社）

【PBOC, other regulators back expansion of offshore trade finance pilot in Shanghai FTZ Lingang New Area】The PBOC and other regulators' Action Plan directs optimizing offshore trade finance services and supports expanding the comprehensive offshore trade finance reform pilot in the Shanghai FTZ Lingang New Area. 【PBOC and regulators to prudently pilot offshore finance in Pudong】The People's Bank of China and other regulators will prudently and orderly launch an offshore financial pilot in Shanghai's Pudong New Area to boost the district's role in global financial resource allocation. The pilot will test concentrated offshore activity using physical clustering, restricted eligible entities and account segregation to allow moderate aggregation while isolating risk. It targets provision of offshore services — payments and settlement, FX conversion, investment and financing, insurance and asset management — to Chinese 'going out' firms' overseas subsidiaries and qualifying Belt and Road and other foreign enterprises. Shanghai is encouraged to establish standards, processes and supervisory rules for accessing offshore services; authorities will study the feasibility of setting up offshore banks and other offshore financial institutions.

Milestones: by end-2027 establish business rules, risk-management and resolution frameworks and an offshore-friendly operating environment, and pilot digital-frontier offshore financial businesses to better serve Chinese firms’ outbound activity. By end-2030 form a relatively mature offshore institutional and legal framework to provide secure financial services for Chinese international investment and trade, serve as a testbed for onshore financial reform, and support RMB asset global allocation and risk-management functions. By end-2035 position Shanghai as a strategic hub coordinating high-level onshore–offshore development and leading national financial opening and high-quality growth.

【潘功勝：持續建設規範、開放、有活力、有韌性的現代化金融市場】中國人民銀行行長潘功勝在2026陸家嘴論壇表示，進一步建設現代化金融市場，是“十五五”時期的一項重要任務。站在新的歷史起點，中國金融市場將堅持市場化、法治化、國際化的發展方向，持續建設規範、開放、有活力、有韌性的現代化金融市場。重點聚焦以下五個方向。第一，建設和發展多類別、多層次的金融市場體系，進一步豐富金融市場業態。第二，持續深化市場化改革，進一步發揮金融市場價格發現和資源配置作用。第三，健全法治規則體系，進一步培育良好的市場生態。第四，完善金融市場宏觀審慎管理框架和監管體系，進一步提升金融市場執行穩定性和韌性。第五，穩步推進金融領域制度型開放，進一步提升中國金融市場的國際化水平。人民銀行將加強開放條件下金融穩定保障體系和能力建設，守住不發生系統性風險的底線。（金融一線）

Palladium Steadies After 8-Month Low — Palladium hovered near $1,350 per ounce, steadying after an eight-month low as easing Middle East tensions boosted risk appetite across commodity markets. Brent crude futures fell below $80 a barrel after dropping nearly 5% in the previous session following the announcement of an interim US-Iran peace deal. Falling oil prices led investors to scale back expectations for further Fed tightening, with the probability of a December rate hike dropping to about 60% from roughly 70% a week earlier. Lower rate expectations weighed on Treasury yields and supported precious metals. Additional support came from short covering, while investors continued to monitor supply risks and the outlook for automotive demand. Market participants are awaiting a series of central bank meetings this week, including the Fed's rate decision on Wednesday, the first under new Chair Kevin Warsh. Over the past month, palladium has fallen 3.84%, but remains 30.39% higher than a year ago.

### 2026-06-17 10:48:55  #宏觀 #國際

【叢林法則捲土重來 多邊主義失靈了嗎？王毅回應】今天（6月17日），國務院新聞辦公室舉行新聞釋出會，釋出《構建更加公正合理的全球治理體系：中國的理念、倡議與行動》白皮書。總檯央視記者提問：越來越多的人正在擔憂當前的國際秩序是正在退回到弱肉強食的叢林法則，同時聯合國的權威和作用也在受到削弱，在這樣的背景之下，中國將如何維護多邊主義以及如何支援聯合國更好地發揮作用？中共中央政治局委員、外交部長王毅作出回應。不是聯合國不重要 而是沒有得到尊重和維護80多年前正是因為看到叢林法則橫行造成的人類浩劫，國際社會才在兩次世界大戰的廢墟上痛定思痛，選擇擁抱多邊主義，創立了聯合國。今天，多邊主義之所以看似失靈，不是由於聯合國不再重要，而恰恰是由於聯合國的權威和地位沒有得到尊重和發揮。叢林法則之所以捲土重來，不是由於聯合國的憲章過時了，而恰恰是因為憲章沒有得到有效的遵守和維護。面對全球挑戰 當務之急是履行聯合國憲章義務面對層出不窮的全球挑戰，當務之急就是要履行聯合國憲章的義務，奉行主權平等、遵守國際法制、反對強權霸凌，要積極支援聯合國發揮核心作用，透過聯合國這個平臺來凝聚全球共識，協調各國行動，共同應對挑戰，不斷消弭全球治理的短板和赤字。同時，我們還要與時俱進，加快聯合國改革的步伐，積極回應發展中國家的訴求，著力提升全球南方國家的代表性和發言權，持續激發聯合國的活力，提升聯合國的效能。中國願同各國一道重振聯合國，壯大聯合國作為第一個在聯合國憲章上簽字的國家——中國，我們責無旁貸願意透過全球治理倡議的推進來維護聯合國的權威，同各國一道重振聯合國、壯大聯合國。（央視新聞）

### 2026-06-17 10:47:25  #宏觀 #原創

【潘功勝：金融市場韌性增強，經受住了多輪次、高強度的外部衝擊】中國人民銀行行長潘功勝在2026陸家嘴論壇表示，金融市場現代化是中國經濟體制改革的重要組成部分，是中國改革開放的生動縮影。三十多年來，特別是黨的十八大以來，中國金融市場逐步從最初以信貸市場為主，發展成為包括股票、債券、貨幣、黃金、票據、大宗商品、衍生品等各金融子市場協同發展、功能健全、種類完備的現代金融市場體系，有力助推了中國經濟穩定增長、轉型升級和高質量發展。市場深度廣度大幅提升，國際地位與影響力實現跨越式提升。股票市場，從上世紀90年代初上交所最開始的8只股票，到目前A股上市公司5500家，總市值超過110萬億元，位居全球第二。債券市場，從1997年銀行間債券市場成立時餘額不足5000億元，到目前總規模突破200萬億元，穩居世界第二。外匯市場，已覆蓋全球主要經濟體超40種外幣，年交易量超42萬億美元。發行、交易等市場化機制全面建立。利率、匯率市場化改革不斷深化。中小板、創業板、科創板等多層次資本市場持續完善。證券交易所、中央國債公司、外匯交易中心、上海黃金交易所、上海清算所等金融市場基礎設施體系基本建成，中國主要的金融市場基礎設施基本都佈局在上海。金融市場韌性增強，經受住了多輪次、高強度的外部衝擊。雙向開放穩步推進，深度融入全球金融體系。放開外資金融機構准入，引入QFII、RQFII等長期境外資金，推出滬深港通、債券通、互換通、ETF互通等跨境互聯互通機制。人民幣債券、股票被納入明晟（MSCI）、富時羅素、彭博巴克萊等全球主流指數，境外機構和個人持有境內人民幣金融資產超過10萬億元人民幣。（金融一線）

### 2026-06-17 10:42:23  #央行 #宏觀

【中國人民銀行行長潘功勝在2026陸家嘴論壇上宣佈即將出臺的政策措施】一是完善短端利率調控機制。為進一步推動貨幣政策框架向價格型轉型，增強短端利率調控的精準性和有效性，人民銀行將對利率調控機制進行進一步探索和最佳化：一方面，在2024年7月設立臨時隔夜正/逆回購工具的基礎上，完善工具使用機制，並將操作利率調整為7天期逆回購操作利率加、減25個基點，區間由70個基點收窄為50個基點。另一方面，進一步豐富公開市場操作工具箱，適時增加隔夜逆回購操作品種，更好地匹配銀行體系短期的流動性需求。
二是創設境外央行回購工具。設立境外央行類機構回購工具，包括境外央行或貨幣當局、國際金融組織、主權財富基金，都可以用中國國債等高等級債券回購的方式，從人民銀行獲得人民幣流動性，便利境外央行類機構的人民幣流動性管理和人民幣資產配置。
三是在上海自貿區開展離岸人民幣外匯交易試點。為推動外匯市場雙向開放，促進在岸和離岸市場融合，助力上海打造人民幣資產全球配置中心和風險管理中心，人民銀行將授權工行、農行、中行、建行、交行、中信等6家銀行，利用中國外匯交易中心平臺在上海自貿區開展離岸人民幣外匯交易。未來，人民銀行將根據試點情況，進一步推動離岸人民幣外匯交易市場發展。
四是研究設立特定情景非銀流動性支援宏觀審慎工具。研究政策工具，當債券等市場出現系統性壓力，正常流動性渠道受阻，機構群體性面臨流動性危機且可能引發系統性風險時，透過互換方式向非銀機構提供緊急流動性。機制設計將綜合權衡維護金融市場穩定執行和防範金融市場道德風險。“特定情景”意味著該機制不是對非銀金融機構的常態化流動性供給；“防範金融市場道德風險”意味著非銀機構與央行操作必須滿足宏觀審慎要求，還要提供高等級抵押品。
五是會同上海市政府和有關部門出臺《上海國際金融中心發展離岸金融行動方案》。人民銀行將逐步健全適應離岸金融的業務規則、風險管理、營商環境等制度體系，穩妥推進自貿離岸債、離岸貿易金融服務、國際財資中心等離岸金融業務，支援上海率先構建與國際金融中心相匹配的離岸金融業務體系。
六是銀行間市場資料包告庫正式掛牌。透過集中收集交易、託管、結算等資料資訊，提升對金融市場的穿透監測能力，服務於監管，服務於市場。（金融一線）

### 2026-06-17 10:35:47  #Trading Economics #Markets #Indonesia #Stock Market #Importance 1

Indonesia Stocks Trade at Near One-Month Peak — Indonesia’s IDX Composite climbed 55 points or 0.9% to 6,312 in early Wednesday trade, marking a near one-month high and its third straight gain as traders returned from a holiday. Stronger U.S. stock futures boosted sentiment after Wall Street ended mixed overnight ahead of the Fed’s policy decision later today under new chairman Kevin Warsh. The central bank is widely expected to hold rates steady, with Warsh seen potentially scrapping the ‘dot plot’ to give policymakers more flexibility. A firmer rupiah also lent support, aided by hopes of Middle East peace that lifted risk appetite and pressured the U.S. dollar index. Still, upside was capped after Fitch Ratings warned that local commodity exporters face higher credit risks under Indonesia’s centralized export policy. All sectors advanced, led by basic materials, non-cyclicals, infrastructure, and energy. Notable movers included Aneka Tambang (2.9%), Bank Central Asia (2.8%), Astra Agro Lestari (2.5%), and United Tractor (1%).

【高層建築消防治理再出重拳 多部門將部署開展專項整治工作】今天（6月17日）上午，國家消防救援局舉行例行新聞釋出會，通報了近期消防安全形勢和消防救援重點工作。國家消防救援局相關負責人表示，近期部分地區發生高層建築火災，引發社會廣泛關注，將會同有關部門，持續深化高層建築消防安全治理，堅決整治消防設施癱瘓、控制室無人值守、消防管網無水、防火分隔不到位、佔用消防通道等突出問題隱患，強化人防物防技防措施，提升滅火救援能力。國家消防救援局將重點抓好四方面工作：一是壓實各方責任，推動相關部門強化行業安全管理，督促物業按照合同約定把消防服務做到位；二是全面排查整治，聚焦高層建築外牆保溫、豎向管井等高風險部位，重點糾治違規動火施工、消防設施損壞、安全出口上鎖、消防控制室值班脫崗、電動腳踏車進樓入戶等突出問題；三是有效應急處置，督促管理單位建強微型消防站、志願消防隊，確保滅早、滅小、滅初期；四是加強宣傳警示，選取典型火災案例以案說法，強化全社會消防安全意識。

【國家禁毒辦介紹中美禁毒合作情況】今天上午（6月17日）國家禁毒辦召開新聞釋出會。國家禁毒辦常務副主任、公安部禁毒局局長魏曉軍介紹中美雙方禁毒合作情況及下步合作展望。一是雙方開展了很多機制性交流和務實合作，我們與美國白宮國家禁毒政策辦公室等政策部門保持聯絡溝通，定期相互通報工作進展，商討下步合作優先事項；與美國緝毒署、聯邦調查局和移民海關執法局等執法部門聯合偵辦多起案件，合作遣返涉毒逃犯，具體情況已對外發布。二是為積極合作應對非列管化學品流入北美製毒渠道問題，2025年11月，商務部等五部委聯合釋出公告，將13種芬太尼類物質前體化學品和美國、墨西哥、加拿大分別增列到《向特定國家（地區）出口易製毒化學品管理目錄》《特定國家（地區）目錄》，今年5月又針對上述三國增列了3種芬太尼類物質前體化學品；2025年11月和今年5月，國家禁毒辦連續兩次釋出警示通告，提醒相關企業和個人在從事相關經營活動時嚴格遵守相關國家法律法規，防範前體化學品和裝置流入製毒渠道；海關部門加強了對相關品種化學品的出口監管和風險分析，提醒出口企業識別客戶、防範風險；郵政部門敦促寄遞企業嚴格落實“實名收寄、收寄驗視、過機安檢”三項制度，持續開展宣傳教育，切實提升寄遞行業從業人員對重點敏感化學品的防範意識。三是針對國內少數不法分子向境外販運列管或非列管前體化學品及新精神活性物質等敏感化學品的情況，在全國範圍內多輪次部署開展打擊管控專項行動，全面強化案件偵辦。去年12月中國石油和化學工業聯合會還主辦了“清朗網路空間·共築禁毒防線”全國化工行業淨網倡議活動，有效提高行業自律，取得良好效果。

Australia 10Y Yield Hits 15-Week Low — Australia’s 10-year government bond yield dropped below 4.8%, hitting a fifteen-week low as global oil prices continued to slide on optimism over the reopening of the Strait of Hormuz. The United States and Iran are expected to sign an interim peace agreement in Switzerland on Friday, which is seen restoring oil flows through the Persian Gulf and easing inflation concerns. In Australia, the Reserve Bank signaled further tightening to restrain inflation, despite leaving interest rates unchanged at its June meeting. While the RBA opted to pause to assess the full impact of three rate hikes earlier this year, Governor Michele Bullock emphasized that inflation remains too high and reiterated that further rate increases cannot be ruled out. The hawkish tone reinforced bets for another hike this year, with markets now pricing in around a 30% of an August move and a roughly 60% odds of one final hike to a peak of 4.6%. The upcoming May CPI report due next week will be pivotal for the outlook.

Japan 10Y Yield Slips Despite Strong Trade Data — Japan’s 10-year government bond yield slipped to around 2.6% on Wednesday, giving back some of the previous session’s gains despite stronger-than-expected trade data and a recent interest rate hike by the central bank. Official figures showed Japanese exports jumped 17% year-on-year in May, marking the fastest growth since November 2022, supported by solid demand for automobiles and semiconductors. The data followed the Bank of Japan’s decision on Tuesday to raise its policy rate by 25 basis points to 1% in an effort to contain inflation and support the weakening yen. However, the move faced opposition from some board members, with Toichiro Asada arguing that downside risks to economic growth and employment outweigh the upside risks to inflation.

Duty-exempt processing value for domestic sales under the processing value-added policy was CNY 580 mln, with CNY 30.25 mln in duty relief. Bonded upstream-downstream processing value-added concessions and inclusion of on-island self-produced goods’ value-added have been fully implemented. ### 2026-06-17 10:19:06  #宏觀

【白皮書呼籲：在歷史演進的重大關頭攜手前行】國務院新聞辦公室17日釋出的《構建更加公正合理的全球治理體系：中國的理念、倡議與行動》白皮書指出，當今世界進入新的動盪變革期，人類又面臨和平還是戰爭、對話還是對抗、共贏還是零和的抉擇。令人鼓舞的是，在國際社會廣泛支援下，全球治理倡議日益成為各方積極參與的實踐。偉大事業不會一蹴而就，落實倡議需要各方不為風雨所阻，不為紛爭所困，攜手構建更加公正合理的全球治理體系。白皮書說，全球治理關乎世界各國人民共同和長遠利益，本質是謀公義，而不是做交易。中國呼籲，各國要以人類前途為懷，以人民福祉為念，切實將倡議付諸行動，建立利在千秋、福澤萬民的長久之功。白皮書說，構建更加公正合理的全球治理體系，是可行可及的美好目標，也是一個歷史過程。中國認為，只要各方立定堅韌不拔之志，不捨銖積寸累之功，一定能將願景建設成實景。要做自強不息的奮進者、搏擊者，不做等待觀望的猶豫者、懈怠者，把改革完善全球治理的偉業不斷向前推進。白皮書說，全球治理追求的是國際社會共同福祉，依靠的是國際社會共同努力。中國主張，各國應團結合作，起而行之，把全球治理倡議的遠景和藍圖變成各國攜手行動的路線圖和時間表。團結就是力量。團結合作才能辦成事、辦好事、辦大事，才能把全球治理倡議的主張轉化為最廣泛共識和最有力行動。白皮書指出，這是一個充滿挑戰又充滿希望的時代，更美好的未來需要一代接一代為之奮鬥。這是一個承載理想又腳踏實地的倡議，更完善的全球治理需要一程又一程向前推進。展望未來，隨著國際社會越來越多地瞭解和參與這一重大倡議，堅持多邊主義的進步力量必將不斷壯大，構建更加公正合理全球治理體系的宏偉目標必將一步步成為現實。

【農業農村部：今年夏糧豐收已成定局 產量形勢明朗】從農業農村部瞭解到，夏糧是糧食生產的第一季，據農情排程，夏糧主產省抓住晴好天氣加快搶收，全國夏糧收穫過九成、大頭落地。面積上，應對去年秋季連陰雨影響，主產區積極落實抗溼播種和晚播應變技術，基本做到應播盡播，夏糧面積穩住了，奠定了生產基礎。單產上，主產區因墒因苗運籌肥水，促進弱苗轉化升級；大力落實鎮壓劃鋤、“一噴三防”、病蟲防控等大面積單產提升技術措施，加之溫光水匹配，苗情追回，小麥每畝穗數增加明顯，奠定了單產提升的基礎，特別是7000多萬畝的旱地小麥，單產比去年大幅提升。今年“三夏”期間，全國投入各類農機具超1700萬臺（套），其中聯合收割機80餘萬臺、參與跨區作業20餘萬臺，組織投入履帶式收割機28萬餘臺、烘乾機7萬餘臺開展應急搶收搶烘。麥收省份設立跨區作業接待服務站3100餘個，高速公路開通農機綠色通道5500餘個，公佈省市縣三級24小時農機作業服務熱線1300餘個，確保“機到有人接、遇到困難有人管”。總的看，今年夏糧面積保持穩定，單產有望提升，產量形勢明朗，豐收已成定局。

### 2026-06-17 10:09:56  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China #Hang Seng Index #Impact mixed #CSI300

【China financial regulator vows to eliminate supervisory gaps and blind spots, ensure full coverage with no exceptions】At the 2026 Lujiazui Forum, China's financial regulator Director Ding Xiangqun said authorities will strengthen oversight to eliminate supervisory gaps and blind spots and ensure full coverage with no exceptions. He said priority is preventing and resolving risks to firmly guard against systemic financial stress. Authorities will focus on shrinking legacy exposures and curbing new risk growth, and will orderly but forcefully resolve risks at small- and medium-sized financial institutions. The regulator will support and coordinate measures to address property-sector and local-government debt risks. Emphasis will be on upstream prevention: establishing early-correction mechanisms with hard constraints to enable early identification, early warning, early exposure and early resolution. Regulators will intensify both oversight of lawful activity and crack down harder on illegal financial conduct, strengthen central-local coordination and inter-agency linkage, maintain high-pressure enforcement and full-chain systemic governance, and safeguard household deposits and savings.

China Stocks Split as Lujiazui Forum Looms — The Shanghai Composite slipped 0.3% to 4,081, marking its second consecutive daily loss, while the Shenzhen Component rose 0.2% to 15,712 for its third straight session of gains, as investors cautiously awaited policy signals from Beijing to counter economic imbalances. Attention is focused on the high-profile Lujiazui Forum on June 17 to 18, where China’s central bank chief and top financial regulators are expected to hint at fresh economic stimulus. The event comes on the heels of bleak May economic data, which showed domestic consumer spending and investment plummeting to pandemic-era lows, leaving surging exports as a temporary cushion. While last year’s forum focused on boosting outbound investment and yuan futures trading, the annual event is shifting its spotlight to technology this year. Financial heavyweights led the decliners, particularly Industrial and Commercial Bank of China (-2%), China Construction Bank (-2.1%), and Agricultural Bank of China (-1.1%).

【白皮書：全球治理倡議為破解難題提供時代答案】國務院新聞辦公室17日釋出的《構建更加公正合理的全球治理體系：中國的理念、倡議與行動》白皮書指出，當今世介面臨嚴峻複雜的危機挑戰，日益嚴峻的挑戰呼喚更加有效的治理，叢林法則嚴重衝擊國際法治，彌補治理體系短板勢在必行，全球南方聲音需要更多傾聽。全球治理倡議為破解難題提供時代答案。 白皮書指出，全球治理倡議以維護聯合國憲章宗旨和原則、踐行共商共建共享的全球治理觀為基本遵循，提出奉行主權平等、遵守國際法治、踐行多邊主義、倡導以人為本、注重行動導向五大核心理念，切中諸多危機挑戰的癥結和根源，為構建更加公正合理的全球治理體系提供科學指南。 白皮書說，主權平等是聯合國憲章開宗明義提出的基本原則，是規範國與國關係最重要的準則。各國體量有大小、國力有強弱、發展有先後，但都是國際社會平等一員，主權和尊嚴都必須得到尊重，都有權在全球治理程序中平等參與、平等決策、平等受益。 白皮書說，國際法治是全球治理的根本保障。唯有基於法治的全球治理體系，才能真正為各國提供公平公正的發展環境，確保權利與義務的平衡，推動國際秩序朝著更加公正合理的方向演進。 白皮書說，多邊主義為和平與發展事業而生，多邊機制對於解決全球性問題具有重要意義。沒有哪個國家能置身全球治理體系之外實現發展，也沒有哪個國家能脫離國際合作而贏得未來。多邊主義不是一道選擇題，而是唯一可行路徑。 白皮書說，人類福祉是全球治理的核心關切，各國人民是全球治理的根本參與者和受益者。只有以人民的獲得為目標，不斷為民眾提供信心和穩定預期，全球治理體系才能得到廣泛支援並有效運作。 白皮書說，全球治理的活力源於實踐，關鍵在於行動，根本要看成效。美好願景要靠實幹來實現，國際社會要錨定共同目標，立足解決問題，採取一致行動。（新華社）

Australia Shares Edge Higher as Fed Decision Nears — Australian equities rose modestly on Wednesday morning session, with the benchmark ASX 200 hovering near 8,945 after a muted prior session. Sentiment was lifted by firmer U.S. futures ahead of the Federal Reserve’s first policy decision under new Chair Kevin Warsh later today. Locally, the Reserve Bank held its cash rate steady at 4.35% on Tuesday, as expected, noting the impact of three hikes since January but keeping the door open to further tightening should inflation persist. However, gains were capped by official warnings that an El Niño in Australia has formed in the Pacific and could intensify into one of the strongest in decades by late 2026. Gains in consumer stocks, non-energy minerals, and logistics helped offset weakness in energy, retail, and transport. Early standouts included Evolution Mining (up 2.9%), Mineral Resources (2.8%), Wisetech Global (2.2%), and Sonic Healthcare (1.0%). The four major banks added between 0.3% and 1%, contributing to the modest advance.

【Jarden: RBA cannot easily use rate moves to accelerate disinflation】Jarden economists warn the Reserve Bank of Australia (RBA) cannot meaningfully speed the natural decline in inflation through interest-rate adjustments. They say inflation composition matters more than headline level and forecast core inflation will stay above 3% until H2 2027. Large upside surprises have been driven mainly by fuel costs—external and outside officials' control—rather than domestic overheating. Jarden says this pressure should ease if Middle East tensions normalize, but flags the key market risk is the extent to which cost pressures pass through to Australian goods and services.

【美伊諒解備忘錄14點內容披露（一）】北京時間17日，美國彭博社和沙烏地阿拉伯阿拉比亞電視臺披露了美國和伊朗達成的諒解備忘錄的14點內容。但內容尚未得到美伊官方證實。其內容全文如下：1. 伊朗與美國，連同其在當前戰爭中的盟友，茲於簽署本諒解備忘錄之際，宣佈包括黎巴嫩在內的所有戰線立即永久停止戰爭，並承諾即日起，彼此不採取任何敵對行動，各自放棄使用武力或威脅使用武力。最終協議將確認本條款及以下各條款的規定。2. 伊朗與美國承諾互相尊重主權和領土完整，互不干涉內政。3. 伊朗與美國承諾，在最長60天的期限內進行談判並達成最終協議，該期限經雙方同意可延長。4. 本諒解備忘錄簽署後，美國立即解除海上封鎖，停止對伊朗的任何干涉或阻撓行為，並在最長30天內將航運恢復至全部運力；伊朗方面的船舶通行量應恢復至戰前水平。美國同時承諾，在最終協議簽署後30天內從周邊地區撤軍。5. 本諒解備忘錄簽署後，考慮到排除技術障礙和水雷清除作業的需要，伊朗將立即採取措施，確保30天內波斯灣至阿曼灣雙向商船通行量恢復至戰前水平。6. 美國承諾，同其地區夥伴一道，制訂一項雙方同意的全面計劃，用於伊朗的重建和經濟發展，並確保提供至少3000億美元的融資。該計劃的執行機制是最終協議的一部分，將在60天內製訂完成。7. 美國承諾，按照最終協議中將要商定的時間表，終止當前對伊朗的所有型別的制裁，包括聯合國安理會和國際原子能機構理事會的決議，以及美國所有一級和二級單邊制裁。

### 2026-06-17 09:58:37  #Trading Economics #Markets #Japan #Currency #Importance 1

Yen Remains Under Pressure — The Japanese yen traded near 160.4 per dollar on Wednesday, remaining under pressure despite stronger-than-expected trade data and a recent interest rate increase by the central bank. Official figures showed Japan’s exports surged 17% year-on-year in May, the fastest pace since November 2022, supported by robust demand for automobiles and semiconductors. The data followed the Bank of Japan’s decision on Tuesday to raise its policy rate by 25 basis points to 1% in an effort to contain inflation and support the weakening currency. However, the move was not unanimously supported, with board member Toichiro Asada arguing that downside risks to growth and employment outweigh the upside risks to inflation. The yen has continued to face selling pressure in recent weeks as traders piled on short positions and engaged in carry trades, reflecting the still-significant interest rate differential between Japan and the US.

【受相關資源端供應緊缺影響 LPDDR5X/4X及渠道DDR5價格全面調漲】伺服器市場對儲存的虹吸效應持續發酵，仍具備供貨配額的消費類客戶正尋求原廠包括次級DRAM在內的各類資源供應，產能有限下未來一段時間記憶體儲廠商獲取上游資源將愈發艱難，本週LP5X/LP4X及渠道DDR5記憶體條全面調漲。自Grace CPU選用LPDDR5X作為記憶體配置之後，英偉達Vera CPU繼續沿用該方案，AMD亦明確跟進，其下一代Verano CPU確認首次引入LPDDR5X。隨著基於LP5X的伺服器CPU解決方案陸續實現量產並進入大規模商用推廣階段，LP5X正加速向伺服器市場進一步滲透。在面對伺服器市場對高頻率LP5X的應用需求呈現顯著增長的情況下，海外原廠也正持續將更多的LP5X產能向伺服器市場進一步傾斜，相應的面向手機、PC等消費類終端的供應快速收緊。 (快閃記憶體市場)

### 2026-06-17 09:44:14  #Trading Economics #Economy #Jordan #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Jordan Inflation Hits Over 3-Year High — The inflation rate in Jordan accelerated to 2.83% year-on-year in May 2026, the highest since April 2023, from 2.49% in April, driven primarily by higher fuel prices. The country raised fuel prices by 11% in April, the first increase since the Middle East conflict began, and partially adjusted fuel prices in May. Transportation costs were the main contributors to May inflation, accounting for 1.23 percentage points, followed by rental costs (0.68 percentage points) and oils and fats (0.27 percentage points). For the first five months of the year, inflation averaged 1.88%, down from 1.97% in the same period of 2025, with rental costs (0.68 percentage points) and transportation (0.26 percentage points) being the main contributors. On a monthly basis, CPI rose 0.55% in May, easing from a 0.70% increase in April and marking the softest increase in three months.

### 2026-06-17 09:39:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Leading Economic Index #Importance 1

Australia Leading Index Subdued in May — The Westpac–Melbourne Institute Leading Economic Index was flat month-over-month in May 2026 after an upwardly revised 0.1% gain in the previous month, underscoring weak momentum. Meanwhile, the six-month annualised growth rate edged to -0.17% from -0.18% in April, signalling below-trend growth likely through late 2026 into early 2027. While no sharp downturn is evident, the drag from higher rates and elevated fuel costs is expected to build gradually. At its June meeting, the Reserve Bank paused to gauge the impact of earlier hikes and the energy shock but warned inflation risks remain skewed upward, leaving scope for further tightening. Consistent with the leading Index, growth appears subdued, though inflation remains the central bank’s primary concern. Westpac expects upcoming CPI to remain uncomfortably high, raising the odds of additional hikes in the coming months.

【中國網際網路金融協會網際網路貸款自律工作委員會籌備組釋出“踐行金融為民理念，引領網際網路貸款行業健康發展”首期舉措】6月17日，據中國網際網路金融協會訊息，中國網際網路金融協會網際網路貸款自律工作委員會籌備組制定了“踐行金融為民理念，引領網際網路貸款行業健康發展”系列舉措，度小滿、螞蟻科技、騰訊、京東科技、抖音等籌備組成員單位將做好表率、嚴格執行。倡導廣大從業機構主動對標，以更高標準強化自我約束，攜手履行社會責任，彰顯行業溫度與擔當。現釋出首期7條舉措：一、對新市民、靈活就業人員、“兩司兩員”、小微企業、個體工商戶、農戶、脫貧人口等群體進行力所能及的幫扶，給予更優惠的貸款條件。二、建立紓困機制，對因家庭生活重大變故、臨時性資金週轉困難等陷入困境的客戶，會同合作金融機構視情況給予停催、展期、停息、減息、重組等措施。三、加大紓困投入，全體參與機構每年紓困投入合計不低於15億元。同時，研究由行業相關機構共同出資設立行業紓困基金的必要性和可行性。四、加強幫扶紓困資訊共享和成果披露，共建跨機構協同約束機制和基礎設施，提升對困難群體的識別能力，避免出現“一方接受紓困、一方新增借貸”的行為。五、用好異常資訊共享系統，防範相關黑灰產影響，保障幫扶紓困措施真正惠及廣大金融消費者。六、加強行業幫扶紓困研究，制定行業幫扶紓困人群識別標準、各項措施適用條件和範圍，推動行業幫扶紓困長期可持續發展。七、加大行業幫扶紓困正面宣傳力度，及時總結推廣典型幫扶紓困案例，樹立行業良好形象。

### 2026-06-17 09:38:35  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Korea #Stock Market #Importance 1

South Korean Shares Pause Four-Day Rally — The benchmark KOSPI edged down to around 8,710 on Wednesday, pausing a four-day rally, as weakness in global technology stocks and caution ahead of the Federal Reserve's policy decision weighed on sentiment. Semiconductor shares came under pressure after a selloff in US AI-related stocks, with market heavyweight Samsung Electronics declining and weighing on the broader index. Losses were also seen in Hyundai Motor (-3.9%), Kia Corporation (-2.2%), Hyundai Mobis (-4.1%), and KB Financial Group (-1.7%). Investors also remained wary ahead of the first Federal Open Market Committee meeting under Fed Chair Kevin Warsh, amid concerns that policymakers could signal a more hawkish stance despite expectations that interest rates would remain unchanged. Meanwhile, easing tensions in the Middle East and receding oil-price pressures supported risk appetite and helped limit losses.

### 2026-06-17 09:37:56  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Currency #Importance 1

Aussie Holds Firm on RBA Hawkish Outlook — The Australian dollar held above $0.70, sustaining its rebound from a recent two-month low as the Reserve Bank signaled further tightening to restrain inflation. After the central bank left the cash rate unchanged at 4.35% at its June meeting, Governor Michele Bullock said the board remains alert to upside inflation risks, stressing that demand still needs to moderate further. While the RBA opted to pause in order to assess the full impact of three rate hikes earlier this year, she reiterated that further rate increases cannot be ruled out. The hawkish tone reinforced bets for another hike this year, with markets now pricing in around a 30% chance of a rate increase at the August meeting and a roughly 60% probability of one final hike to a peak of 4.6%. The upcoming May CPI report due next week will be pivotal for the outlook. Meanwhile, improving risk sentiment from progress toward a US-Iran peace deal also supported the Aussie, easing concerns over energy-driven inflation pressures.
